This truth is admittedly not very pleasant, but that is how it is, and at least we can understand why things are as they are. And it seems to make a lot of sense—compared to, for example, the formula produced by the superpowerful computer in Douglas Adams’s famous novel The Hitchhiker’s Guide to the Galaxy. After thousands and thousands of years of processing the question “What is the meaning of life?,” the computer finally comes up with the answer, which is: 42. So—compared with this, Lacan’s formula is literally bursting with sense or, more precisely, with the capacity to make sense of our miseries.

这样一来，非关系就被（错误地）当成了终极真理，当成了现实的终极密码或公式。这个真理诚然不太令人愉快，但事情就是这样；至少我们能明白，事情为什么会是现在这个样子。而且它看起来很有道理——比如，与道格拉斯·亚当斯那本著名小说《银河系漫游指南》里那台超级强大的计算机给出的公式相比，就更是如此。那台计算机处理“生命的意义是什么？”这个问题，处理了千千万万年，最后终于给出答案：42。所以——和这个答案相比，拉康的公式简直意义满溢；更准确地说，它满溢着一种能力：为我们的苦难赋予意义，让我们的苦难变得可理解。

In this “understanding,” we are thus led to conclude that the non-relation is the cause of the oddities and difficulties within all concrete relationships. More precisely: the ontologically stated non-relation is seen in this perspective as the obstacle to the formation of any “successful” concrete, empirical relationship. Lacan’s point, however, is paradoxically almost the opposite: it is only the inexistence of the relation that opens up the space for relationships and ties as we know them. In Lacan’s own words: “the absence of the relation does of course not prevent the tie (la liaison), far from it—it dictates its conditions” (Lacan 2011, 19). The non-relation gives, dictates the conditions of, what ties us, which is to say that it is not a simple, indifferent absence, but an absence that curves and determines the structure with which it appears. The non-relation is not the opposite of the relationship, it is the inherent (il)logic (a fundamental “antagonism”) of the relationships that are possible and existing.

在这种“理解”中，我们于是被引向这样的结论：非关系是所有具体关系内部的怪异之处与困难的成因。更确切地说：从这个视角看，在本体论上被表述的非关系，被视为任何“成功的”具体、经验性关系得以形成的障碍。然而，拉康的要点悖论地几乎相反：正是关系的不存在，才为我们所知的关系与纽带打开了空间。用拉康自己的话说：“关系的不存在当然不阻止纽带（la liaison），远非如此——它规定纽带的条件”（Lacan 2011, 19）。非关系给出并规定那联结我们的东西的条件；也就是说，它并非单纯、无关紧要的缺席，而是一种会弯曲并规定与它一同显现的结构的缺席。非关系不是关系的对立面，而是可能且现存的关系所固有的（非）逻辑（一种根本性的“对抗”）。c2_2

note：这个弯曲其实是拓扑学的含义，就像爱和恨拓扑学上在一个平面一样。不是“因为非关系存在，所以关系失败了”；而是“恰恰因为性关系不存在，各种具体的经验关系才有可能被创造出来”

This represents a new and original conceptual model of the discursive space as generated out of, and around, a missing link in the ontological chain of its own reality. Biased by its constitutive negativity, this structure is always more or less than what it is, that is to say, more or less than the sum of its elements. Moreover, the causal link between these (signifying) elements is determined by what appears at the place of this negativity as both heterogeneous to and inseparable from the signifying order: the impossible substance of enjoyment, conceptualized by Lacan in terms of the (partial) object a. Object a is not a sexual object. Rather, it is a-sexual. It is the objective counterpart of the non-relation (we could say that it is non-relation as object). Yet it is also what is at work in all forming of ties, in the very structuring of (discursive) being qua being. With this in mind it is more than a pun, a play on words, to suggest that what follows from this Lacanian conceptualizations is an “object-disoriented ontology.” If there is an ontology that follows from psychoanalytic (Lacanian) theory, this can only be an ontology as “disoriented” by what he calls the object a.

这代表了一个新的、原创性的话语空间概念模型：它由自身现实的本体论链条中的一个缺环所生成，并且围绕这个缺环生成。受其构成性否定性所偏斜，这个结构总是多于或少于它之所是，也就是说，总是多于或少于其元素之和。此外，这些（能指）元素之间的因果联系，由出现在这一否定性位置上的东西所决定；这东西既异质于能指秩序，又与能指秩序不可分离：它就是享乐的不可能实体，拉康用（部分）对象a来概念化它。对象a不是性对象。更准确地说，它是非性的（a-sexual）。它是非关系的客观对应物（我们可以说，它就是作为对象的非关系）。然而，它也总是在一切纽带的形成中起作用，甚至就在（话语性）存在之为存在的结构化本身中起作用。由此可以说，从这些拉康式概念化中引出的是一种“对象—失向的本体论”；这就不只是双关或文字游戏了。如果确实有一种本体论可以从精神分析（拉康）理论中推出，那它只能是一种被他所说的对象a“弄得失向”的本体论。

So, again, what is most valuable in the Freudo-Lacanian concept of sexuality is that it introduces a conceptual model of thinking the non-relation as dictating the conditions of different kinds of ties, including social ties (or discourses).

所以，再次强调，弗洛伊德-拉康的性概念最有价值的地方在于：它引入了一个概念模型，让我们能够把“非关系”思考为规定各种联结——包括社会联结（或话语）——之条件的东西。c2_3

It is in this precise sense that one could reaffirm the well-known slogan “the sexual is political” and give it a new, more radical meaning. “The sexual is political” not in the sense of sexuality as a realm of being where political struggles also take place, but in the sense that a true emancipatory politics can be thought only on the ground of an “object-disoriented ontology” as sketched above—that is, an ontology that pursues not simply being qua being, but the crack (the Real, the antagonism) that haunts being from within, in-forms it.

正是在这个确切意义上，人们可以重申“性的即政治的”这一众所周知的口号，并赋予它一种新的、更彻底的含义。“性的即政治的”，并不是说性是一个存在领域，政治斗争也在这个领域里展开；而是说，真正的解放政治只能建立在如前所述的“对象—失向的本体论”之上——也就是说，这种本体论所追索的，不只是存在之为存在，而是那个从内部纠缠存在、并内在地给存在赋形的裂口（实在界、对抗）。

In what follows I will develop this with reference to an example which will help us explore and articulate more closely what is at stake in these claims. The example is that of a most peculiar encounter between sexuality and politics as staged in an ingenious text by the Russian Marxist author Andrei Platonov, “The Anti-Sexus,” situated at the very heart of the twentieth century’s discussions of a possible emancipatory politics.

接下来，我会借助一个例子来展开这一点。这个例子能帮助我们更切近地探索并阐明，这些主张究竟牵涉什么。这个例子，是性与政治之间一次极为奇特的相遇，它在俄罗斯马克思主义作者 Andrei Platonov 那个别出心裁的文本《The Anti-Sexus》中被搬演出来；而这个文本，正处在20世纪关于一种可能的解放政治的讨论的核心。

The Anti-Sexus

In his introduction to the recent English (re)publication of Andrei Platonov’s “The Anti-Sexus,” Aaron Schuster made the following observation:

If part of the twentieth century’s revolutionary program to create a radically new social relation and a New Man was the liberation of sexuality, this aspiration was marked by a fundamental ambiguity: Is it sexuality that is to be liberated, delivered from moral prejudices and legal prohibitions, so that the drives are allowed a more open and fluid expression, or is humanity to be liberated from sexuality, finally freed from its obscure dependencies and tyrannical constraints? Will the revolution bring an efflorescence of libidinal energy or, seeing it as a dangerous distraction to the arduous task of building a new world, demand its suppression? In a word, is sexuality the object of or the obstacle to emancipation? (Schuster 2013, 42)

Aaron Schuster 在为安德烈·普拉东诺夫《The Anti-Sexus》最近英文版（重新出版）所写的导言中，作了如下观察：

如果说，二十世纪创造一个彻底全新的社会关系和一个新人的革命纲领，其中一部分就是性的解放，那么这一诉求本身就带着一种根本的含混：要被解放的究竟是性本身——把它从道德偏见和法律禁令中解放出来，让冲动得以更开放、更流动地表达——还是人类要从性中被解放出来，最终摆脱性那些晦暗的依赖和暴虐的束缚？革命会带来力比多能量的迸发，还是会把性视为对建设新世界这一艰巨任务的危险干扰，并要求压制它？一句话，性是解放的对象，还是解放的障碍？（Schuster 2013, 42）

Schuster is quite right to suggest that this may be a mistaken alternative, in the sense that it misses something crucial about the psychoanalytic take on sexuality—as well as, we may add, about its take on emancipation. Whereas emancipation is most often conceived in terms of freeing ourselves from the (social) non-relation—or as approaching the Ideal of the Relation, even if it is unattainable—Lacan presents us with a very different perspective. The aim to abolish the non-relation (and to replace it with a Relation) is, rather, the trade- mark of all social repression. Sexual difference and the oppression of women are very good examples of this. The most oppressive societies have always been those which axiomatically proclaimed (enforced) the existence of the sexual relation: a “harmonious” relation presupposes an exact definition of essences (involved in this relation) and of roles pertaining to them. If there is to be a relation, women need to be such and such. A woman who doesn’t know her place is a menace to the image of the relation (as a totality of two elements that complement each other, for example, or as any other kind of “cosmic order”). To this psychoanalysis does not respond by saying that woman is in fact something other than what these oppressive orders make her out to be, but with a very different, and much more powerful, claim: Woman does not exist. (We shall return to this later on, in our discussion of the sexual difference or divide.)

Schuster 很正确地说，这可能是一个错误的替代选项，因为它漏掉了精神分析对性的把握中某个关键的东西——我们还可以补一句，也漏掉了精神分析对解放的把握中某个关键的东西。解放最常被设想成从（社会）非关系中解放出来——或者被设想成趋近关系的理想，哪怕这个理想无法达到——但拉康给我们的却是一个很不一样的视角。废除非关系（并代之以关系）这一目标，恰恰是一切社会压抑的标志。性差异和对女人的压迫就是很好的例子。最具压迫性的社会，总是那些以公理方式宣告（并强制执行）性关系存在的社会：一种“和谐的”关系，预设了对（参与这一关系的）各种本质以及与之相关的各种角色作出精确界定。如果要有关系，女人就得是某某样子。一个不知道自己的位置的女人，对关系的形象——比如关系作为两个相互补充的元素的总体，或任何别的“宇宙秩序”——就是一种威胁。对此，精神分析并不回应说：女人实际上并不是这些压迫性秩序所说的那样；它提出的是一个非常不同、也强大得多的主张：女人不存在。（后文讨论性差异或性分野时，我们会再回到这一点。）

If we look at the history of political (and class) oppression, we can also see how the enforced idea of a “harmonious” system or social organism has always been accompanied by the most brutal forms of exclusion and oppression. The (Lacanian) point, however, is not simply something like: “Let’s acknowledge the impossible (the non-relation), and instead of trying to ‘force’ it, rather, put up with it.” This, indeed, is the official ideology of the contemporary “secular” form of social order and domination, which has abandoned the idea of a (harmonious) totality to the advantage of the idea of a non-totalizable multiplicity of singularities forming a “democratic” network. In this sense it may even seem that the non-relation is the dominant ideology of “capitalist democracies.” We are all conceived as (more or less precious) singularities, “elementary particles,” trying to make our voices heard in a complex, non-totalizable social network. There is no predetermined (social) relation, everything is negotiable, depending on us and on concrete circumstances. This, however, is very different from what Lacan’s non-relation claim aims at. Namely: the (acknowledged) absence of the relation does not leave us with a pure pluralistic neutrality of (social) being. This kind of acknowledging of the non-relation does not really acknowledge it. What the (Lacan­ian) non-relation means is precisely that there is no neutrality of (social) being. At its most fundamental level, (social) being is already biased. The non-relation is not a simple absence of relation, but refers to a constitutive curving or bias of the discursive space—the latter is “biased” by the missing element of the relation. In this sense, to conceive democracy, for example, as a more or less successful negotiation between elements of a fundamentally neutral social being is to overlook—indeed, to repress—this consequential negativity, operative at the very core of the social order. It is in fact just another form of the narrative of the relation, which becomes quite clear if we think about how the political and economic ontology of the non-totalizable multiplicity of neutral singularities is usually accompanied by the idea of some kind of self-regulation. “The invisible hand of the market” is the showcase example of this.

如果我们回顾政治（以及阶级）压迫的历史，也能看到：那种被强加的“和谐”体系或社会有机体的观念，总是伴随着最残酷的排斥和压迫形式。不过，拉康的要点并不是简单地说：“让我们承认不可能（非关系），然后不要试图‘强行’建立关系，而是忍受它。”这其实是当代“世俗”形式的社会秩序与支配的官方意识形态：它放弃了（和谐的）总体性观念，转而主张一种不可总体化的独异性多元性，由这些独异性构成一个“民主”网络。在这个意义上，非关系甚至看起来像是“资本主义民主”的主导意识形态。我们都被设想为（或多或少珍贵的）独异性、“基本粒子”，试图在一个复杂、不可总体化的社会网络中让自己的声音被听见。没有预先决定的社会关系，一切都可以协商，取决于我们和具体处境。但这与拉康的非关系主张非常不同。也就是说：关系（被承认）的缺席，并不会把我们留在一个纯粹多元主义的中性社会存在里。 这种对非关系的承认，其实并没有真正承认它。拉康意义上的非关系恰恰意味着：不存在社会存在的中性。在最基本的层面上，社会存在已经是有偏斜的。非关系不是简单的关系缺席，而是指话语空间的构成性弯曲或偏斜——后者被关系中缺失的元素所“偏斜”。在这个意义上，比如把民主设想为根本上中性的社会存在诸要素之间或多或少成功的协商，就是忽视——甚至压抑——这种在社会秩序最核心处运作、具有后果的否定性。事实上，它只是关系叙事的另一种形式；只要想想，中性独异性的不可总体化多元性这一政治与经济本体论，通常如何伴随着某种自我调节的观念，就很清楚。“市场看不见的手”就是其典型例子。

note：举例：劳资关系其实本身并非中立，它本身已经被资本主义弯曲了；大数据的推荐算法本身也并非中立，而是偏向于极端、对立、情绪化的言论。

For Lacan, the non-relation is a priori in the precise sense that it appears with every empirical relation as inherent to its structure, and not as its other. The choice is never that between relation and non-relation, but between different kinds of relations (bonds) that are being formed in the discursive space curved by the non-relation. The non-relation does not mean that there is no (fixed, predetermined) relation between particular elements, but refers to a declination, a twist in these elements themselves: “in themselves” these elements already bear the mark of the non-relation (and this mark is the surplus-enjoyment adhering to them). Acknowledging the non-relation does not mean accepting “the impossible” (as something that cannot be done), but seeing how it adheres to all things possible, how it in-forms them, what kind of antagonism it perpetuates in each concrete case, and how. This is the kind of acknowledgment that—far from closing it—only opens up the space of political invention and intervention.

对拉康来说，非关系是先验的，严格说来，它的意思是：非关系随每一种经验关系一同出现，内在于这种关系的结构，而不是作为它的他者。选择从来不是在关系与非关系之间，而是在不同种类的关系（纽带）之间；这些关系在由非关系所弯曲的话语空间里形成。非关系并不意味着特定元素之间没有（固定的、预先规定的）关系，而是指这些元素自身的一种偏转、一种扭转：“就其自身而言”，这些元素已经带有非关系的标记（而这个标记就是附着于它们的剩余享乐）。承认非关系，并不意味着接受“不可能”（仿佛它是做不到的事），而是要看它如何附着于一切可能之物，如何内在地形塑它们，在每一个具体情形中它维持的是何种对抗，以及如何维持。这种承认远非关闭它，反而恰恰打开了政治发明与介入的空间。

But let us return to the “The Anti-Sexus” and to how it can help us see and define the core of the problem. So, what is this text? To sum it up, I will rely once more on Schuster’s presentation:

In 1926, Russian Marxist author Andrei Platonov composed The Anti-Sexus, a remarkable text which remained, like so many of his other writings, unpublished during his lifetime. The work is a fictional brochure, issued by the company Berkman, Chateloy, and Son, Ltd. and “translated” from French by Platonov, that advertised an electromagnetic instrument promising to relieve sexual urges in an efficient and hygienic manner. The device, available in both male and female models, had a special regulator for the duration of pleasure and could be fitted for either personal or collective use. The purported occasion for the pamphlet was the company’s expansion into the Soviet market after its success in many other parts of the world. The brochure includes a statement touting the virtues of the “Anti-Sexus” and the company’s mission to “abolish the sexual savagery of mankind,” and is followed by testimonials by a number of illustrious figures, from Henry Ford and Oswald Spengler to Gandhi and Mussolini. The Anti-Sexus, we are told, has many benefits and applications: it is perfect for maintaining soldiers’ morale during wartime, for improving the efficiency of factory workers, for taming restless natives in the colonies. It also fosters true friendship and human understanding by taking sexual folly out of the social equation. The “translator” has added a critical preface where he condemns the cynicism and vulgarity of the enterprise, even while praising the pamphlet’s writerly merits. He explains that the reason he decided to publish the text was to openly reveal the bourgeoisie’s moral bankruptcy. No Bolshevik can read this capitalist drivel without a hearty laugh. The Anti-Sexus thus advertises itself as the surest form of “contra-‘antisexual’ agitprop.” (Schuster 2013, 42–45)

但让我们回到《反性爱机》（The Anti-Sexus），回到它如何帮助我们看见并界定问题的核心。那么，这个文本究竟是什么？为了概括它，我再借助 Schuster 的陈述：

1926年，俄国马克思主义作家 Andrei Platonov 写了《反性爱机》（The Anti-Sexus）。这是一部引人注目的文本，像他许多其他作品一样，生前未能发表。这部作品是一本虚构的广告册，由 Berkman, Chateloy, and Son, Ltd. 公司发行，并由 Platonov “译自法文”；它为一种电磁仪器做广告，承诺以高效而卫生的方式缓解性冲动。这种装置有男用和女用两种型号，带有一个控制快感持续时间的特殊调节器，并且可以装配成个人使用或集体使用。据称，这本小册子问世的契机，是该公司在世界许多地区取得成功后，正要把市场扩展到苏联。小册子里有一则声明，吹捧“反性爱机”的优点，以及公司“废除人类性野蛮”的使命；随后是若干显赫人物的推荐语，从 Henry Ford、Oswald Spengler 到 Gandhi、Mussolini，不一而足。据我们被告知，“反性爱机”有许多好处和用途：它非常适合在战时维持士兵士气，提高工厂工人的效率，驯服殖民地躁动不安的土著。它还能把性愚行从社会方程中剔除出去，从而促进真正的友谊和人类理解。这位“译者”加了一篇批评性序言，谴责这一企划的犬儒和粗俗，同时又称赞小册子的写作优点。他解释说，自己决定出版这个文本，是为了公开揭露资产阶级的道德破产。任何一个布尔什维克读这种资本主义的胡言乱语，都不可能不开怀大笑。因此，《反性爱机》把自己标榜为最可靠的一种“反‘反性’宣传鼓动”。（Schuster 2013, 42–45）

We will not go into the (very interesting) question of where Platonov stands in this debate, staged as it is by him in a multilayered and multigenre way, in which a text of literary fiction is presented as a translation of an advertising pamphlet accompanied by comments by prominent men (yes, they are all men) and by a critical introduction from the alleged “translator.” We will simply take the text at face value, and start by interrogating the presuppositions and paradoxes of the device (called the Anti-Sexus) advertised and discussed in the alleged pamphlet.

我们不去讨论一个（很有意思的）问题：Platonov在这场辩论中站在哪一边——这场辩论由他本人以多层次、多文类的方式编排出来，其中一部文学虚构文本被呈现为一份广告小册子的翻译，还配有显要人物（没错，全是男人）的评论，以及据称的“译者”所写的批判性导言。我们只按文本表面来读，先从这份所谓小册子所宣传和讨论的装置（名为Anti-Sexus）的预设与悖论开始追问。

These are the presuppositions of the Anti-Sexus device: Sexuality is problematic because it involves the Other who, as everybody knows, is utterly unpredictable, unreliable (has her own will, caprices, indispositions …) or simply unavailable. On the other hand, and at the same time, our relations with others are complicated and conflict-ridden because expectations and demands concerning sex are always in the air, complicating things: sex stands in the way of good social relations. This is the double quandary presumably resolved by the Anti-Sexus device, which is claimed to be able to isolate, extirpate what is sexual about enjoyment, from all other pleasures and relations in which it appears—to distillate, as it were, the pure essence of sex (and then administer it in just the right dosages). In this way, the Anti-Sexus provides an “Other-free” enjoyment (enjoyment free of the Other) and at the same time makes it possible for us to relate to others in a really meaningful way: to create real, lasting bonds (pure spiritual friendship).

这些就是 Anti-Sexus 装置的预设：性之所以成问题，是因为它牵涉大他者，而众所周知，大他者完全不可预测、不可靠（她有自己的意志、任性、身体不适……），或者干脆不可得。另一方面，与此同时，我们与他人的关系之所以复杂、充满冲突，是因为关于性的期待和要求总悬在空气里，把事情搞复杂：性妨碍了良好的社会关系。这就是 Anti-Sexus 装置大概要解决的双重困境。据说它能从享乐中把属于性的东西隔离、切除出来，使之脱离它出现于其中的所有其他快感和关系——可以说，就是蒸馏出性的纯粹本质（然后以恰到好处的剂量加以施用）。这样一来，Anti-Sexus 就提供了一种“无须大他者”的享乐（摆脱大他者的享乐），同时也让我们有可能以真正有意义的方式与他人发生关系：建立真实、持久的联结（纯粹的精神友谊）。

It is clear that two operations are at stake here, or two aims: on the one hand, the aim is to extract sex from the Other; on the other hand, it is to exempt the Other from sex. In this way we get two separate entities: as the result of the first operation, we get a sexless Other (to whom one can now relate in a friendly and non-problematic way); as the result of the second operation, we get a pure substance of sex, which we can enjoy directly whenever we want to.

显然，这里涉及两种操作，或者说两个目标：一方面，目标是从大他者中把性抽离出来；另一方面，是让大他者免于性。这样一来，我们就得到两个彼此分离的实体：作为第一种操作的结果，我们得到一个无性的大他者（现在人们可以以一种友好、无碍的方式与之建立关系）；作为第二种操作的结果，我们得到一种纯粹的性实体，我们可以随时直接享乐它。

The Anti-Sexus is said to accomplish both things:

We have been called upon to solve the global human problem of sex and the soul. Our company has transformed sexual feeling from a crude elemental urge to an ennobling mechanism, we have given the world moral behavior. We have removed the element of sex from human relationships and cleared the way for pure spiritual friendship.
Still, keeping in mind the high-value instant pleasure that necessarily accompanies contact of the sexes, we have endowed our instrument with a construction affording a minimum of three times this pleasure, as compared to the loveliest of women used at length by a prisoner recently released after ten years in strict isolation. (Platonov 2013, 50)

据说，Anti-Sexus能同时达成两件事：

我们受命去解决性欲与灵魂这一全人类问题。我们公司已经把性感受从一种粗糙的原初冲动改造成一种使人高尚的机制，我们给了世界道德行为。我们已经把性的元素从人际关系中抽离出去，为纯粹的精神友谊铺平了道路。
不过，考虑到两性接触必然伴随高价值的即时快感，我们也给器具设计了一种构造，使它至少能提供这种快感的三倍——哪怕拿一个在十年严格隔离后刚刚获释、随后长时间享用最漂亮女人的囚犯来比，也是如此。（Platonov 2013, 50）

As much as we can be tempted to laugh here, this addresses a problem that has been all but constantly raised in modern debates concerning the possible (and radical) emancipation of humankind: the crucial obstacle to global human emancipation is humanity (“human nature”) itself. Human emancipation is actually emancipation from the human. Human nature is the weak link in the project of social emancipation. In this line of thought we usually get a harder and a softer mode of resolving this dilemma: either to build a New Man, or to “canalize” the disruptive factor of humanity, and “satisfy” it in a way that cannot interfere with building and maintaining the social Relation.

尽管我们在这里很可能会忍不住发笑，但这里所触及的问题，在现代关于人类可能（且彻底）解放的争论中几乎一直不断被提出：全人类解放的关键障碍，就是人类（“人性”）本身。人的解放实际上就是从“人”中解放出来。人性是社会解放工程中的薄弱环节。沿着这种思路，我们通常会得到解决这一困境的两种方式，一硬一软：要么造就一个新人，要么把人性中的破坏性因素“疏导”，并以一种不干扰社会关系之建立与维持的方式去“满足”它。2_4

The proposition of the Anti-Sexus is to canalize the disruptive element. But this is the problem: can this “disruptive element” really be thought of in terms of an element, that is, in terms of something that one can define, circumscribe, isolate? The answer seems to be no, and this is most evident in the way in which the basic operation of the Anti-Sexus immediately falls into two different operations: extracting, removing sex from the Other, and exempting, removing the Other from sex. Not much is said about how the first is done; the device basically provides the method of the second. It exempts the Other from sexual pleasure, and the idea is, it would seem, that this automatically accomplishes the other task as well: it extracts, removes sex from the Other, or produces a sexless Other, ready to form spiritual bonds with me. Since the sexual needs of the Other are always perfectly satisfied, she or he becomes sexless (sex is not a player in the relationship between people). This, of course, is a strange presupposition, to say the least. The Other is sexless if he or she “is being masturbated” most of the time.

Anti-Sexus 的主张，是把那个扰乱性要素加以疏导。可问题就在这里：这个“扰乱性要素”真的能作为一个要素来想吗？也就是说，能作为某种可定义、可界定、可孤立出来的东西来想吗？答案似乎是否定的；这一点最清楚地表现在，Anti-Sexus 的基本操作立刻分裂成两种不同的操作：把性从大他者中抽取出来、移除掉；以及把大他者从性中豁免出去、排除出去。关于第一种操作如何进行，说得不多；这个装置基本上提供的是第二种的方法。它把大他者从性快感中豁免出来，而其设想似乎是：这也会自动完成另一项任务——把性从大他者中抽取、移除，或者生产出一个无性的大他者，随时准备与我建立精神纽带。既然大他者的性需要总是被完美满足，她/他就变得无性了（性不再在人与人的关系中起作用）。当然，至少可以说，这是一个奇怪的前提。如果大他者“大部分时间都在被手淫”，他/她就是无性的。

And here we come to the very “matheme” of the Anti-Sexus device, which I propose to formulate as follows: “to make oneself masturbated,” “se faire masturber”—to paraphrase the grammatical form used by Lacan in his conceptualization of the drive.5 In order to properly conceptualize the drive as something that escapes the active/passive opposition, Lacan proposes a formula that introduces something active at the very heart of passivity, and vice versa. In the case of the scopic drive, for example, he dismantles what looks like reversal(s) between seeing and being seen with the formulation making oneself seen. In this sense the Anti-Sexus and its formula (“to make oneself masturbated”) could be said to provide the formula of the nonexistent “sexual drive.” We saw how its task is actually twofold and twisted: in order to remove enjoyment from the Other, one has to remove the Other from enjoyment. This suggests in fact that enjoyment and the Other are structured like a matryoshka: enjoyment is “in” the Other, but when we look “in” the enjoyment, there is also the Other “in” it, and so on. … Enjoyment is in the Other, and the Other is in enjoyment. —This is perhaps the most concise formulation of the structure of the non-relation, the non-relation between the subject and the Other. If enjoyment is what disturbs this relation, it does so not simply by coming between them (and hence holding them apart), but rather by implicating, placing them one in the other.

于是，我们就来到 Anti-Sexus 装置最核心的“数学型”。我建议把它表述为：“让自己被手淫”（to make oneself masturbated），“se faire masturber”——这是对拉康在把冲动概念化时所使用的语法形式的改写。2_5为了恰当地把冲动理解为某种逃出主动/被动对立的东西，拉康提出一个公式，在被动性的正中心引入某种主动的东西，反过来也一样。比如在观看冲动中，他用“让自己被看见”这一表述，拆解了看与被看之间看起来像是倒转的关系。在这个意义上，可以说 Anti-Sexus 及其公式（“让自己被手淫”）提供了那个并不存在的“性冲动”的公式。我们已经看到，它的任务实际上是双重的，也是扭曲的：为了从大他者那里移除享乐，就必须从享乐中移除大他者。这其实暗示，享乐和大他者像俄罗斯套娃一样被结构起来：享乐在“大他者里”，但当我们往享乐“里”看时，里面也有大他者，如此等等。……享乐在大他者之中，大他者也在享乐之中。——这或许是主体与大他者之间非关系的最简洁表述，也就是非关系的结构。如果享乐是扰动这一关系的东西，那么它并不是单纯地来到两者之间（因而把它们隔开），而是让两者彼此卷入、把一个放进另一个之中。

note：其实举个例子，比如 Anti-Sexus 虽然移除了肉体接触的过程并保证了性快感（从大他者那里移除了享乐），但是其本身作为倒错式的自慰行为却又一直保证着大他者的不在场的在场（自反性的凝视等等。。），于是 Anti-Sexus 无法从享乐中将大他者移除掉——以为保证了纯净的享乐，结果享乐内部依旧是大他者。

Let us take a moment here and look more closely at both sides of this configuration.

我们在这里稍作停留，更仔细地看看这个构型的两个方面。

What we have on the one hand is this: all enjoyment already presupposes the Other, regardless of whether we “get it” with the help of the “real other” (another person) or not. This is Lacan’s fundamental point. Even the most solitary enjoyment presupposes the structure of the Other. This is also why, the more we try to get rid of the Other and become utterly self-dependent, the more we are bound to find something radically heterogeneous (“Other”) at the very heart of our most intimate enjoyment. There is no enjoyment without the Other, because all enjoyment originates at the place of the Other (as the locus of the signifiers). Our innermost enjoyment can occur only at that “extimate” place. (And this is not the same as saying that enjoyment is mediated by the Other, or that we “need” the Other in order to enjoy.) It is of the utmost importance to grasp that the radical heterogeneity, incommensurability, and antagonism between the signifier and enjoyment is not due to their heterogeneous origins (for example, that one comes from the body and the other from the symbolic order), but on the contrary to the fact that they originate at the same place. The Other is both the locus of the signifier and the locus of enjoyment (mine, as well as the enjoyment of the Other).

一方面，我们面对的是这一点：一切享乐都已经预设了大他者，不管我们是否借助“真实的他人”（另一个人）“得到它”。这是拉康的根本观点。哪怕是最孤独的享乐，也预设了大他者的结构。也正因如此，我们越是试图摆脱大他者、变得完全只靠自己，就越必定会在我们最私密的享乐的核心，遇到某种根本异质的东西（“他者”）。没有大他者就没有享乐，因为一切享乐都起源于大他者的位置（作为能指的场所）。我们最内在的享乐只能发生在那个“外隐”的位置。（这并不等于说，享乐是由大他者中介的，或者我们为了享乐而“需要”大他者。）最关键的是要把握住：能指与享乐之间根本的异质性、不可通约性和对抗性，并不是因为它们来源异质（比如一个来自身体，另一个来自象征秩序），而恰恰是因为它们起源于同一个位置。大他者既是能指的场所，也是享乐的场所（我的享乐，以及大他者的享乐）。

On the other hand (and as we saw in chapter 1), what we find, for example, at the very heart of the most sex-free, spiritual (Christian) love is a proliferation of partial objects and their enjoyment. It is not pure enjoyment, “enjoyment for the sake of enjoyment,” that is being banned in this discourse; what is banned, or repressed, is the link between enjoyment and sexuality.

另一方面（正如我们在第一章所见），比如，在最不涉性、最精神性的（基督教）爱的核心，我们发现的恰恰是部分对象及其享乐的增殖。在这个话语中被禁止的，并不是纯粹的享乐，即“为享乐而享乐”；被禁止、或被压抑的，是享乐与性之间的联系。

But why exactly? Because this link exposes the non-relation at the very heart of every relation. Like all religions, Christianity presupposes and enforces the Relation. The idea of a “nonsexual sexual enjoyment” that we find here is actually the same as the one at work in the Anti-Sexus device. What is needed for the Relation to exist is a “sexless sex,” or an “otherless Other” (an Other free of otherness).

但究竟是为什么呢？因为这一纽带恰恰暴露出：非关系就处在每一种关系的最核心。像所有宗教一样，基督教预设并强制推行“关系”。我们在这里看到的“非性的性享乐”，其实与“Anti-Sexus device”中运作的那种观念是一回事。要让关系存在，所需要的恰恰是一种“无性的性”，或者一个“无他者的大他者”（一个免除了他者性的大他者）。

This, then, is the double paradox that we are trying to formulate: if we remove the Other from enjoyment, we find the Other at the very heart of the most autofocused, masturbatory enjoyment. On the other hand: if we remove enjoyment from the Other, we find enjoyment at the very heart of the (most spiritual) bond with the Other. The Other and enjoyment are “extimately” related. This is why, in order to remove enjoyment from the Other, a second operation is immediately called for: that of removing the Other from enjoyment. The two “elements” imply each other, each carries the other “in” itself, and this is what twists what may look like a symmetry (or relation) in a way that resembles some of Escher’s drawings of impossible objects.

那么，这就是我们要试着说清的双重悖论：如果我们把大他者从享乐中抽走，就会在最自我聚焦、最手淫式的享乐的核心处发现大他者。反过来：如果我们把享乐从大他者中抽走，就会在与大他者（最具精神性的）联结的核心处发现享乐。大他者与享乐是以“外隐”的方式联系在一起的。正因如此，为了把享乐从大他者中抽走，马上就需要第二个操作：把大他者从享乐中抽走。这两个“要素”彼此蕴含，每一个都把另一个“带在”自身之中；正是这一点扭结了那看似对称（或关系）的东西，方式近似于埃舍尔那些不可能物体的画。

“The Invisible ‘Handjob’ of the Market”

Lacan’s point is that, since it is one with the discursive order, the non-relation is at work in all forms of social bond; it is not limited to the “sphere of love.” (The latter is, rather, distinguished by the fact that in its field it actually happens, from time to time, that the relation “stops not being written.”) And his further point is that the social relations of power—domination, exploitation, discrimination—are first and foremost forms of exploitation of the non-relation.

拉康的意思是，既然非关系与话语秩序是一体的，那么它就在一切社会联结形式中起作用；它并不局限于“爱的领域”。（后者反而特殊：在它的场域里，关系确实会时不时地“停止不被书写”。）他进一步指出，权力性的社会关系——支配、剥削、歧视——首先是对非关系的利用形式。

This is a delicate point, for it seems to contradict a point made earlier: namely, that the most authoritarian social orders are those which aim at freeing the social from the non-relation, that is to say, social orders built in the name of the Relation. Yet this is not necessarily in contradiction with exploiting the non-relation. Perhaps we even find here a good way of distinguishing between the abolition of the non-relation as emancipatory project, and what we may call “narratives of the Relation” which are actually in the service of the most vicious (social and economic) exploitation of the non-relation. Abolition of the non-relation has been in fact the way in which the authentic revolutionary projects of the twentieth century often understood the path to radical emancipation. The catastrophic results of this kind of politics were inherent in the very honesty of the will to abolish the non-relation. The modus operandi of engineering a New order (and a New man) has been that of exposing the non-relation and attempting to force it out of the social equation, by all possible means. And this is very different, in its logic, from what we may call the exploitation and segregation of people by presenting a given form of social antagonism (non-relation) as the ultimate Relation, supposedly protecting us from the utter Chaos of the non-relation. In this way, social injustice directly translates into a higher Justice. At work here is not a crazy attempt to abolish the non-relation as the fundamental negativity, but disavowing it while at the same time appropriating it as the generic (and productive) point of social power. This is a truly political lesson of psychoanalysis: Power—and particularly modern forms of power—works by first appropriating a fundamental negativity of the symbolic order, its constitutive non-relation, while building it into a narrative of a higher Relation. This is what constitutes, puts into place and perpetuates, the relations of domination. And the actual, concrete exploitation is based on, made possible (and fueled) by, this appropriation, this “privatization of the negative.” This is what distinguishes—to take the famous Brechtian example—the robbing of a bank (common theft) from the founding of a bank (a double theft which appropriates the very lever of production and its exploitation).

这是一个微妙之处，因为它似乎与前面提出的一个点相矛盾：最威权主义的社会秩序，恰恰是那些想要把社会从非关系中解放出来的秩序，也就是以“关系”之名建立起来的社会秩序。然而，这未必与利用非关系相矛盾。也许我们甚至可以在这里找到一种好办法，用来区分两种东西：作为解放计划的废除非关系，以及我们可称为“关系叙事”的东西——后者实际上服务于对非关系最恶毒的（社会与经济）榨取。事实上，20世纪真正的革命方案常常就是把废除非关系理解为通向彻底解放的道路。这类政治造成的灾难性后果，恰恰内在于废除非关系这一意志的诚实之中。工程化地制造一个新秩序（和新人）的运作方式，一直是把非关系暴露出来，并试图用一切可能的手段把它从社会等式中强行剔除出去。而在逻辑上，这与我们可称为“通过把某种既定的社会对抗形式（非关系）呈现为终极关系，来剥削和隔离人”的做法非常不同——据说这种终极关系能保护我们，使我们免于非关系的彻底混沌。这样一来，社会不义就直接转化为一种更高的正义。这里运作的，不是一种要把作为根本否定性的非关系废除掉的疯狂尝试；而是对非关系加以否认，同时把它据为社会权力的普遍（且生产性）支点。这是精神分析真正具有政治性的教训：权力——尤其是现代形式的权力——首先占有象征秩序的根本否定性，也就是它构成性的非关系，同时把它编入一个更高关系的叙事之中，以此来运作。正是这一点构成、安置并延续了支配关系。而实际的、具体的剥削，正是以这种占有、这种“否定性的私有化”为基础，并由它成为可能（且被它推动）。正是这一点——以著名的布莱希特式例子来说——把抢银行（普通偷窃）与创办银行（一种双重偷窃，它占有了生产及其剥削的杠杆本身）区分开来。

note：也就是说，避免非关系被发现的方式就是否定非关系，就像恋足癖导致了现在很多的擦边短视频开始给脚打码一样——打码本身蕴含着宏大叙事（绿色互联网秩序、资本主义的暧昧等等）。

Nowhere is this more evident than in the case of capitalism, which starts off with two revolutionary ideas: “the economic Relation does not exist” and “the non-relation could be very profitable.” The first idea corresponds to the eighteenth-century economists, led by Adam Smith, putting into question the previous “mercantile” doctrine and belief that the amount of the world’s wealth remained constant and that a nation could increase its wealth only at the expense of another nation. This is the image of a “closed” totality in which the relation ensures the visibility of the difference (in wealth): if you want more, you have to take it from somewhere, so someone else has to lose. The relation is that of subordination (of the weak to the powerful), but it is still a relation. The new economic idea undermines this (totality-based) relation, while at the same time prizing the productivity of the newly discovered non-relation. The world’s wealth can also increase “by itself,” with the Industrial Revolution and the new organization of labor being the primary sources and carriers of this increase. I am deliberately putting this in the crudest and most simple terms, so as to expose the most salient structural traits of this shift. What is the fundamental “discovery” of capitalism? That non-relation is profitable, that it is the ultimate source of growth and profit. And with this came the idea that, this being so, there is no reason why everybody couldn’t profit from it. This is how we got the narrative of a new, higher Relation, the foundational myth of modern capitalism, known as “the invisible hand of the market.”

没有什么比资本主义的例子更能说明这一点。资本主义一开始就带着两个革命性观念：“经济关系不存在”和“非关系可能非常有利可图。”第一个观念对应的是以亚当·斯密为首的十八世纪经济学家，他们质疑先前的“重商主义”学说和信念：世界财富总量保持不变，一国要想增加财富，只能以另一国受损为代价。这是“封闭的”总体性图像，其中关系保证了差异（财富差异）的可见性：你想多拿，就得从别处拿，所以别人必须失去。这种关系是从属关系（弱者从属于强者），但它仍然是关系。新的经济观念瓦解了这种（基于总体性的）关系，与此同时又看重新发现的非关系的生产性。随着工业革命和新的劳动组织成为这种增长的主要来源和载体，世界财富也可以“自行”增长。我故意用最粗陋、最简单的话来说，以便暴露这一转变最突出的结构特征。资本主义的根本“发现”是什么？非关系是有利可图的，它是增长和利润的终极来源。随之而来的是这样一种观念：既然如此，就没有理由所有人不能从中获利。我们由此得到了一个新而更高的关系的叙事，即现代资本主义的基础神话，被称为“市场看不见的手”。

note：哈耶克的大手。。。

Adam Smith’s “capital” idea starts out from positing a social non-relation as a fundamental state also on another level: as elements of social order, individuals are driven by egotistic drives and the pursuit of self-interest. But out of these purely egotistic pursuits grows a society of an optimal general welfare and justice. It is precisely by ruthlessly pursuing one’s own interest that one promotes the good of society as a whole, and much more efficiently so than when one sets off to promote it directly. As Smith puts it in a famous quote from The Wealth of Nations: “It is not from the benevolence of the butcher, the brewer or the baker, that we expect our dinner, but from their regard to their own self- interest. We address ourselves, not to their humanity but to their self-love, and never talk to them of our own necessities but of their advantages” (Smith 2005, 30).

亚当·斯密的“资本”观念，起点是把社会非关系设定为一种根本状态，并且也在另一个层面上如此：作为社会秩序的元素，个体受利己冲动和自利追求的驱动。可是，从这些纯粹利己的追求中，却生长出一个普遍福利与正义达到最优的社会。恰恰是通过无情地追逐自身利益，人才促进整个社会的善，而且这比一开始就径直去促进它要高效得多。正如亚当·斯密在《国富论》中那句著名的话所说：“我们指望晚餐，不是出于屠夫、酿酒师或面包师的仁慈，而是出于他们对自己利益的考虑。我们求助的不是他们的人性，而是他们的自爱；我们从不跟他们谈我们自己的需要，只谈对他们有什么好处”（Smith 2005, 30）。

What is interesting about this idea in the context of our previous discussion is how it takes a first step in the right direction and then stops short. To put it in the terms we were using earlier, the idea is that what we find at the very core of the most selfish individual enjoyment is actually the Other (looking after a general welfare). What is missing is the next step: and what we find, at the same time, at the core of this Other, is a most “masturbatory” self-enjoyment. Adam Smith’s mistake is not that he saw the dimension of the Other possibly at work in the most selfish pursuits of individual interests—all in all, this thesis is not simply wrong: we never do just what we think we are doing and what we intend to do (this is even a fundamental lesson of both Hegel and Lacan). His mistake was that he did not follow this logic to the end: he failed to see where and how the Other and its invisible hand also do not do only what they think they are doing. … This is what becomes obvious with every economic crisis, and became overwhelmingly clear with the last one: left to itself, the market (the Other) is bound to discover “solitary enjoyment.” At some point in his comments on Platonov’s “Anti-Sexus,” Schuster uses the expression: “the invisible ‘handjob’ of the market,” which I am borrowing here, since one could hardly find a better way of putting what I am trying to articulate. The invisible hand of the market, supposedly looking after general welfare and justice, is always also, and already, the invisible handjob of the market, putting most of the wealth decidedly out of common reach.

在我们先前讨论的语境中，这个想法有意思的地方在于：它朝正确方向迈出了第一步，却中途停住了。用我们前面使用的说法，这个想法是：在最自私的个体享乐的最核心处，我们实际找到的其实是大他者（它在照看普遍福利）。缺失的是下一步：与此同时，在这个大他者的核心处，我们找到的是一种最“手淫式”的自我享乐。亚当·斯密的错误并不在于他看到大他者的维度可能作用于个体利益最自私的追逐之中——总的来说，这个论点并不简单是错的：我们从来不只是做我们以为自己正在做、并且有意去做的事（这甚至是黑格尔和拉康共同的基本教训）。他的错误在于没有把这一逻辑贯彻到底：他没有看到，大他者及其看不见的手，在何处、又如何也并不只做它们以为自己正在做的事。……这一点在每一次经济危机中都变得明显，并在上一次危机中变得压倒性地清楚：一旦任由市场（大他者）自行其是，它必然会发现“孤独的享乐”。Schuster 在评论 Platonov 的《Anti-Sexus》时，某处用了这个说法：“市场那只看不见的‘手淫’”；我这里借用它，因为要表达我想说的东西，几乎找不到更好的说法。市场那只据说照看普遍福利与正义的看不见的手，也总是、并且已经就是市场那只看不见的“手淫”，把绝大部分财富决然地置于大众无法触及之处。

note：没有将认同的点认同到“过度”。

Adam Smith’s idea could indeed be formulated in these terms: Let’s make the non-relation work for everybody’s profit. And one could hardly deny the fact that what we consider as wealth has increased in absolute (and not only relative) terms since the eighteenth century. Or, as we often hear, that everybody, even the poorest, is living better than two centuries ago. Yet the price of this modern economic higher Relation is, again, that the differences (between rich and poor) are also exponentially greater, fed by the non-relation in its “higher” form.

亚当·斯密的想法确实可以这样表述：让非关系运转起来，为所有人谋利。我们也很难否认：自十八世纪以来，我们所认为的财富在绝对意义上（而不只是相对意义上）增长了；或者如人们常说的，所有人，哪怕是最穷的人，也比两个世纪前过得更好。然而，这种现代经济式“更高关系”的代价，同样在于：贫富之间的差异也以指数级扩大，而滋养它的正是以“更高”形式出现的非关系。

Why is the non-relation so productive and profitable? Marx saw it perfectly: in order for the non-relation to be economically productive and profitable, it has to be built into the very mode of production. He situated this at the precise “structural” point when labor appeared on the market as yet another commodity for sale. This is a key point in what he analyzes as “the transformation of money into capital.” To put it very simply: what makes the products (namely, labor power) also appears with them on the market as one of the products, objects for sale. This paradoxical redoubling corresponds to the point of structural negativity and its appropriation as the locus of the market’s “miraculous” productivity. The money-owner finds on the market a commodity whose use value possesses the peculiar property of being a source of value, and whose actual consumption is a creation of value. This is why it is too simple to say that what the capitalists have “more of,” they have “stolen” from the workers. This kind of claim still presupposes the old, “closed,” relation-based economy. … What capital exploits is the point of negativity (“entropy”) of the social order, with the workers situated at this precise point. Capitalists are not so much “stealing” from the workers as employing them to make the negativity/entropy of the system work for them, the capitalists. Or, in other words: “they are making themselves enriched.”

为什么非关系如此具有生产性、如此有利可图？马克思看得很清楚：非关系要在经济上具有生产性、有利可图，就必须被嵌入生产方式本身。他把这一点定位在一个精确的“结构性”点上：劳动力作为又一种可出售的商品出现在市场上。这是他所分析的“货币转化为资本”中的关键点。说得非常简单：让产品成为产品的东西（也就是劳动力），也作为产品之一、作为可售对象，与产品一道出现在市场上。这种悖论性的加倍，对应着结构否定性之点，以及对该点的占有——使之成为市场“奇迹般”生产力的场所。货币持有者在市场上发现一种商品，其使用价值具有一种特殊属性：它是价值的源泉；而它的实际消费就是价值的创造。所以说资本家“多出来的”东西是从工人那里“偷来的”，就太简单了。这种说法仍然预设了旧的、“封闭的”、以关系为基础的经济。……资本所剥削的，是社会秩序的否定性（“熵”）之点，而工人正处在这个精确的点上。资本家与其说是在从工人那里“偷”，不如说是在雇佣工人，让系统的否定性/熵为资本家自己工作。或者换句话说：“他们让自己发了财。”

note：就像前面说的，“把抢银行（普通偷窃）与创办银行区分开来”，资本家雇佣工人，将工人的否定性再一次否定，从而遮蔽了其“不存在”。

This, then, is what Marx recognized as the concrete structural point of the non-relation in capitalism, serving as the condition of its type of production and exploitation. Labor power as commodity is the point that marks the constitutive negativity, gap, of this system: the point where one thing immediately falls into another (use value into source of value). Labor is a product among other products, yet it is not exactly like other products: where other products have a use value (and hence a substance of value), this particular commodity “leaps over” or “lapses” to the source of value. The use value of this commodity is to be the source of value of (other) commodities. It has no “substance” of its own. This could also be put in a formula: “The Worker does not exist.” What exists—and must exist—is the person whose work is sold and bought. This is why it is essential, according to Marx, that the person working does not sell himself (his person), “converting himself from a free man to a slave, from an owner of commodity into a commodity. He must constantly treat his labor-power as his own property, his own commodity” (Marx 1990, 271). This also shows how the usual humanist complaints about how, in capitalism, “we are all just commodities” miss the point: if we were indeed just commodities, capitalism would not work; we need to be free persons selling our labor power as our property, our commodity.

那么，这就是马克思所认识到的资本主义中“非关系”的具体结构点，它充当着其生产与剥削类型的条件。劳动力作为商品，是标记出这个系统之构成性否定性、也就是其裂隙的那个点：在这一点上，一个东西直接落入另一个东西（使用价值落入价值源泉）。劳动是诸多产品中的一个产品，却不完全像其他产品：其他产品有使用价值（因而有价值实体），而这个特殊商品则“跃过”或“滑落”到价值源泉。这个商品的使用价值，就是成为（其他）商品的价值源泉。它没有自己的“实体”。这也可以写成一个公式：“工人不存在。”存在着的——而且必须存在的——是其劳动被买卖的人。因此，在马克思看来，关键在于劳动的人不出卖自己（他的人格），“把自己从自由人变成奴隶，从商品所有者变成商品。他必须始终把自己的劳动力当作自己的财产、自己的商品”（Marx 1990, 271）。这也表明，通常那些人道主义抱怨——说在资本主义里“我们都不过是商品”——如何没抓住要点：如果我们真的只是商品，资本主义就运转不了；我们需要是自由的人，把自己的劳动力当作自己的财产、自己的商品来出售。

note：其实和女人的回溯性构建一样，工人也是回溯性构建出来的。首先其实是无产阶级本身作为什么都不是的阶级成为了一阶的否定关系，其本身是可以揭露社会的裂隙的，但是资本主义赋予了一个身份——你是一个工人你可以通过贩卖劳动力来进入社会秩序——这作为二阶的否定遮盖了裂隙。其实也可以直接用性化公式来分析这个问题：

  1. 条件：不存在一个人不受资本主义逻辑定义——$\neg \exists x \neg \text{Commodity}(x)$
  2. 因为不存在这样的人，所以这个资本主义的集合无法闭合，这导致了非全的集合，也就是无法将这个资本主义逻辑定义的人的集合封闭，于是，并非所有人都受资本主义逻辑定义——$\neg \forall x Commodity(x)$

The Marxian concept of the proletariat could be seen precisely as formulating the fact that, in capitalism, the Worker doesn’t exist (a Worker that existed would actually be a slave). This is why the proletariat is not simply one of the social classes, but rather names the point of the concrete constitutive negativity in capitalism, the point of the non-relation obfuscated and exploited by it. The proletariat is not the sum of all workers, it is the concept that names the symptomatic point of this system, its disavowed and exploited negativity. And this general Marxian idea has lost none of its pertinence today.

马克思的无产阶级概念，恰恰可以被视为表述了这样一个事实：在资本主义中，工人并不存在（一个真正存在着的工人，实际上会是奴隶）。正因如此，无产阶级并不只是诸社会阶级中的一个，而是命名了资本主义中具体的构成性否定性2_6的那个点，命名了被资本主义遮蔽并加以剥削的非关系之点。无产阶级并不是所有工人的总和；它是命名这一系统的症状性点位的概念，命名了该系统所否认并加以剥削的否定性。这一总体的马克思式观念，今天丝毫没有失去它的针对性。

In conclusion, we can return to the invisible hand, its other side and its criticism: is it enough to claim that it does not exist, and to try to put in its place a better, truly operative Other? As a matter of fact, this is precisely the theoretical question that we see today emerging on the left (for example, with Thomas Piketty’s work): is it tenable to play one’s cards on the side of distribution? In other words, is there a way in which we could make the non-relation-based profit really profitable for all (eliminate its “handjob” aspect, as it were)? Can we maintain the profitable side of the non-relation while keeping its negative side under control (by means of different social correctives and regulations concerning the distribution of wealth)?

总之，我们可以回到“看不见的手”、它的另一面及其批判：仅仅声称它不存在，并试图用一个更好、真正有效运作的大他者来取代它，这就够了吗？事实上，这正是我们今天看到在左翼那里浮现出来的理论问题（例如 Thomas Piketty 的著作）：把筹码押在分配这一边，站得住脚吗？换句话说，有没有一种方式，能让以非关系为基础的利润真正惠及所有人（可以说，消除它那种“打手枪”的方面）？我们能否保住非关系中有利可图的那一面，同时把它的负面控制住（借助各种社会矫正手段，以及关于财富分配的规制）？

c2_1. 关于这些问题的全面评注，参见 Dolar 2007，第14–38页。 ↩
c2_2. 这就是为什么，处理性、谈论性，唯一的方式是把它当作一个逻辑问题（或一个本体论—逻辑问题）。这样，我们或许才有机会触及某种实在界。反过来，如果把它当成身体及其感觉的问题，我们就必定落入想象界（或形而上学）。 ↩
c2_3. 另参见 Slavoj Žižek 在 Žižek 2012, 794–802 中对“非关系”的有力讨论。 ↩
2_4. 在某种程度上，较晚近的“后人类”观念也属于这一传统：它把解放设想为本质上就是从“人类”中解放出来。 ↩
2_5. 参见拉康 1987, 194–196。 ↩
2_6. “具体的构成性否定性”这一提法还需要进一步解释。就一般理论而言，对这种构型应当这样说：并不是有一个根本的非关系，外加由它以一种否定方式所决定的多种不同关系。更准确地说，每一种关系也设定了决定它的那个不可能性的具体点。它决定了那个将会决定它的东西。在这个意义上，我们可以说，所有社会关系都是非关系的具体化；非关系是话语的普遍规定，而它除了存在于这些具体的（非）关系之中，不在任何别处存在。这也意味着，非关系不是话语的终极（本体论）基础，而是话语的表面——它只通过话语存在并显现。换种说法：并不是有一个根本的非关系（并且一直持存），任何具体关系都永远无法把它（重新）解决。更准确地说：每一种具体关系事实上都在解决非关系，但它要解决非关系，只能连同自身一起设定（“发明”）自身的否定性、自身的否定性条件/不可能性。非关系不是某种“坚持”并“持存”的东西，而是某种被重复的东西——某种“不停止不被书写”的东西（借用拉康的说法）。它不是某种抵抗一切书写、任何书写实际上都无法书写的东西——它内在于书写，并随书写一起重复自身。（泛神论。。。） ↩

Chapter 3

Contradictions that Matter

Sex or Gender?

Let us recapitulate some of the important points made so far, which will orient us in our further discussion. One of the founding gestures of psychoanalysis was to cut short the discussion of sexuality as a moral question by relating it to an epistemological difficulty, with immanent ontological relevance. What, in fact, is sexuality? Far from chaining humanity to its animal or natural heritage, sexuality is the problematic territory of being that seems to throw us out of joint, disorient us, and make us indulge in things characteristic of human society (politics, art, science, love, religion …). In his early work Freud still played with the simple idea of casting sexuality as the hidden motive: repressed sexuality resurfaces as the driving force behind all kinds of highly spiritual human creations, as well as horrors. Later, he put it in a different perspective, additionally fortified by Lacan and his “return to Freud”: if sexuality is so closely related to the unconscious and to mechanisms of repression, the reason is not its moral controversy, but its paradoxical ontological status, manifesting itself as an epistemological problem, or an epistemological limit. Moral issues surrounding sexuality (its concealment and shame, its oppression, its codification and regulation, its penalization, as well as its liberation, its revealing, displaying and endlessly discussing) have their origin in sexuality as an ontological problem. Sexuality is the paradigm of research and exploration, not in the sense of the reduction to the last instance but, on the contrary, because it brutally introduces us to the lack of the last instance. It is precisely this lack of the last instance that becomes the place of thought, including the most speculative (metaphysical) thinking. And it is no coincidence that the discussion of sexual difference in psychoanalysis, in its most venerable tradition, often sounds or reads like “high mathematics”: formulas, logical paradoxes, complicated formulations, and counterintuitive theses. Theses concerning sexuality are in fact the most speculative (or “philosophical”) part of psychoanalytic theory. It is also no coincidence that the shift from considering sexuality as a moral issue to focusing on its problematic ontological and epistemological status casts sexuality (and particularly sexual difference) as an immediate political problem. Not a cultural problem, not a problem of identity, but a political problem. Not a problem of “human rights,” but a problem of political rights. At its core, feminism has always been a political movement. This is precisely what contemporary ideology tries to make us forget (or else to make us dismiss, precisely because it was political). We get images of “hysterical,” “fanatical,” “masculine,” “wild,” “ideological” suffragettes as opposed to “calm,” “composed” women who see themselves as human beings with specific qualities and an identity, and try to democratically affirm it; women who would start by saying, “I’m not a feminist, I just …” True feminism depends on positing sexual difference as a political problem, and hence on situating it in the context of social antagonism and of emancipatory struggle. Feminism did not start from trying to affirm some other, female identity (and its rights), but from the fact that roughly half of the human race, referred to as “women,” was nonexistent in a political sense. It is this nonexistence, this political invisibility, which actually functioned as a homogeneity of the political space, that feminism transformed into a split, a division, which concerns all (hence its political dimension). In this context it is essential that at stake in this gesture is not a political affirmation of some independently existing ontological divide (between “men” and “women”), but something that first constitutes sexual difference as difference or divide. And it does so by forcing us to think of it as a division, a split of the same world. The traditional division between masculine and feminine worlds (domains, spheres: for example, public/private) actually does not see sexual difference as difference, but as a question of belonging to two separate worlds, which are “different” from a neutral bird’s-eye description, but otherwise coexist as integral parts in the hierarchy of a higher cosmic order, the wholeness and unity of which is in no way threatened by this “difference.” These are parts that “know their place.” And feminism (as a political movement) puts in question, and breaks, precisely this unity of the world, based on massive suppression, subordination, and exclusion. Once again: this exclusion is not an exclusion of female identity; on the contrary, the mythology of female identity is precisely what has made this exclusion possible, and what sustains it. The theme of “female identity” sustains the difference and exclusion on the prepolitical level, on the level of belonging to two different worlds. In this sense, (emancipatory) politics begins with “loss of identity,” and there is nothing deplorable in this loss. Preachers of traditional values usually propagate the political exclusion of women precisely by evoking their (specific) identity. They believe that the Woman exists, and they need Her to exist. Is the right response to this to fill the “Woman” up with different content and to promote her as the Other voice, the voice of alterity, which also needs to be heard and affirmed? No: the political explosiveness of “the woman question” does not lie in any specificity or positive characteristics of women, but in its capacity to inscribe the problem of division and difference into the world the homogeneity of which is based on exclusion. This exclusion—and this point is absolutely crucial—is not simply the exclusion of the other side, or half, but above all the exclusion (“repression”) of the split (social antagonism) as such; it is the erasing of a social antagonism. Its reappearance (in the form of feminist struggle) is the appearance of the social division in the pure state, and this is what makes it political, and politically explosive.

让我们回顾一下到目前为止提出的若干要点，它们将为我们接下来的讨论定向。精神分析的一个奠基性举措，就是打断把性当作道德问题来讨论的做法，转而把它与一种认识论困难联系起来，而这种困难具有内在的本体论相关性。事实上，性是什么？性远没有把人类拴在其动物性或自然遗产上；它是存在中一个成问题的领域，似乎使我们脱节、迷失方向，并使我们沉溺于人类社会的典型事物（政治、艺术、科学、爱、宗教……）。在早期工作中，弗洛伊德还在玩弄一个简单想法：把性设定为隐藏的动机——被压抑的性重新浮现，成为一切高度精神性的人类创造以及种种恐怖的驱动力。后来，他换了一个视角，这一视角又得到拉康及其“回到弗洛伊德”的加强：如果性与无意识和压抑机制如此紧密相关，那么原因并不在于它的道德争议，而在于它悖论性的本体论地位——这种地位表现为一个认识论问题，或一个认识论极限。围绕性的道德问题（它的遮掩与羞耻、它的压迫、它的编码与规制、它的惩罚，以及它的解放、揭示、展示和无休止讨论）都源于作为本体论问题的性。性是研究与探索的范式，这不是把一切还原到最终实例，恰恰相反，因为它粗暴地让我们遭遇最终实例的缺失。正是这种最终实例的缺失，成为思想的位置，包括最思辨的（形而上学）思考。也并非偶然，精神分析中最受尊崇的传统里关于性差异的讨论，常听起来或读起来像“高等数学”：公式、逻辑悖论、复杂表述和反直觉论题。关于性的论题，实际上是精神分析理论中最思辨（或“哲学”）的部分。同样并非偶然，从把性视为道德问题转向关注其成问题的本体论和认识论地位，会把性（尤其性差异）直接投置为政治问题。不是文化问题，不是身份问题，而是政治问题。不是“人权”问题，而是政治权利问题。女性主义在核心上始终是一场政治运动。这正是当代意识形态试图让我们忘记的（或者让我们轻蔑打发，恰恰因为它曾是政治的）。我们得到的形象是“歇斯底里的”“狂热的”“男性化的”“野性的”“意识形态化的”妇女参政论者，与之相对的是“平静”“镇定”的女人：她们把自己视为具有特定品质和身份的人，并试图以民主方式确认它；她们一开口就说：“我不是女性主义者，我只是……”真正的女性主义取决于把性差异设定为政治问题，因而把它置于社会对抗和解放斗争的语境中。女性主义不是从试图确认某种另外的女性身份（及其权利）开始，而是从这一事实开始：被称为“女人”的约一半人类，在政治意义上并不存在。正是这种不存在、这种政治上的不可见——它实际上曾充当政治空间的同质性——被女性主义转化为一种分裂、一种涉及所有人的区分（因此才有其政治维度）。在这个语境中，关键的是：这个举措的要害不是政治地肯定某种独立存在的本体论区分（“男人”与“女人”之间），而是某种首次把性差异构成为差异或分裂的东西。它这样做，是迫使我们把它思考为同一个世界的分裂、区分。传统上男性世界与女性世界（领域、范围：例如公共/私人）的划分，实际上并不把性差异看作差异，而看作归属于两个分离世界的问题；这两个世界从一个中立的鸟瞰描述看是“不同的”，但除此之外，它们作为更高宇宙秩序等级中的组成部分共存，而这个秩序的整体性与统一性绝不会被这种“差异”威胁。这些部分“知道自己的位置”。而女性主义（作为政治运动）恰恰质疑并打破这个世界的统一性，这种统一性建立在大量压制、从属和排斥之上。再次强调：这种排斥不是对女性身份的排斥；相反，女性身份的神话恰恰使这种排斥成为可能，并维持着它。“女性身份”这一主题在前政治层面、在归属于两个不同世界的层面上，维持着差异与排斥。在这个意义上，（解放的）政治始于“身份的丧失”，这种丧失没有什么可悲叹的。传统价值的宣讲者通常正是通过唤起女人的（特定）身份，来传播对女性的政治排斥。他们相信大写女人存在，并且需要她存在。对此的正确回应，是用不同内容填充“女人”，并把她推为大他者之声、他异性之声，说这种声音也需要被听见和肯定吗？不：“女人问题”的政治爆炸力，不在于女人的任何特殊性或正面特征，而在于它能够把分裂与差异的问题写入一个其同质性建立在排斥之上的世界。这种排斥——这一点绝对关键——不简单是对另一方、对另一半的排斥，而最重要的是对分裂（社会对抗）本身的排斥（“压抑”）；它是对社会对抗的抹除。它的重新出现（以女性主义斗争的形式）就是社会分裂以纯粹状态出现，而这正使它成为政治的，并具有政治爆炸力。

Sexual difference is a singular kind of difference, because it starts out not as difference between different identities, but as an ontological impossibility (implied in sexuality) which only opens up the space of the social (where identities are also generated). This ontologically determinative negativity involved in the concept of sexual difference is precisely what is lost with the replacement of this concept with that of “gender differences.” As Joan Copjec forcefully put it:

The psychoanalytic category of sexual difference was from this date [the mid-1980s] deemed suspect and largely forsaken in favor of the neutered category of gender. Yes, neutered. I insist on this because it is specifically the sex of sexual difference that dropped out when this term was replaced by gender. Gender theory performed one major feat: it removed the sex from sex. For while gender theorists continued to speak of sexual practices, they ceased to question what sex or sexuality is; in brief, sex was no longer the subject of an ontological inquiry and reverted instead to being what it was in common parlance: some vague sort of distinction, but basically a secondary characteristic (when applied to the subject), a qualifier added to others, or (when applied to an act) something a bit naughty. (Copjec 2012, 31)

性差异是一种独特的差异，因为它一开始并不是不同身份之间的差异，而是（性中所隐含的）一种本体论上的不可能性；正是这种不可能性才打开了社会空间（身份也在其中被生成）。性差异概念所包含的这种在本体论上具有决定作用的否定性，恰恰在它被“性别差异”替换时丢失了。正如 Joan Copjec 一针见血地指出的那样：

从这时（20世纪80年代中期）起，精神分析中的“性差异”范畴就被视为可疑，并在很大程度上被放弃，转而采用被去势的“性别”范畴。对，就是被去势的。我坚持这个说法，因为当“性差异”被“性别”取代时，恰恰是性差异中的那个“性”掉落了。性别理论完成了一项大功：把性从性里拿掉了。因为性别理论家虽然继续谈论性实践，却不再追问性或者性欲是什么；简言之，性不再是本体论探究的对象，反而退回到日常用语里的样子：某种模糊的区分，但根本上是一种次要特征（当用于主体时），是加在其他特征上的限定语；或者（当用于行为时）是有点下流的东西。（Copjec 2012, 31）

If we want to avoid this move, the crucial question becomes: What exactly does the insistence on sex (and sexual difference) as the subject of an ontological inquiry amount to?

如果我们想避开这一步，关键问题就变成：坚持把性（以及性差异）当作本体论探究的主体，究竟意味着什么？

To even suggest discussing sexual difference as an ontological question might induce—not without justification—strong reluctance, the objection that this discussion would achieve nothing new. Traditional ontologies and traditional cosmologies were strongly reliant on sexual difference, taking it as their very founding, or structuring, principle. Yin–yang, water–fire, earth–sun, matter–form, active–passive—this kind of (often explicitly sexualized) opposition was used as the organizing principle of these ontologies and/or cosmologies, as well as of the sciences—astronomy, for example—based on them. And this is how Lacan could say: “primitive science is a sort of sexual technique.” At some point in history, one generally associated with the Galilean revolution in science and its aftermath, both science and philosophy broke with this tradition. And if there is a simple and most general way of saying what characterizes modern science and modern philosophy, it could be phrased precisely in terms of the “desexualization” of reality, of abandoning sexual difference, in more or less explicit form, as the organizing principle of reality, meant or used to provide its coherence and intelligibility.

哪怕只是提出把性差异当作一个本体论问题来讨论，也可能——而且不无道理地——引起强烈抵触，招来这样的反对：这种讨论不会带来任何新东西。传统本体论和传统宇宙论都高度依赖性差异，把它当作自身最根本的奠基性原则或结构性原则。阴阳、水火、大地—太阳、质料—形式、主动—被动——这类（往往被明确性化的）对立被用作这些本体论和/或宇宙论的组织原则，也被用作以它们为基础的科学（例如天文学）的组织原则。拉康正因此才能说：“原始科学是一种性技术。”3_1 在历史上某个通常与科学中的伽利略革命及其后果联系在一起的时刻，科学与哲学都同这一传统决裂了。如果要用最简单、最一般的方式来说明现代科学和现代哲学的特征，那恰恰可以表述为现实的“去性化”，也就是放弃性差异——无论其形式是明确还是不那么明确——作为现实的组织原则，不再用它来赋予现实以连贯性和可理解性。

The reasons why feminism and gender studies find these ontologizations of sexual difference highly problematic are obvious. Fortified on the ontological level, sexual difference is strongly anchored in essentialism—it becomes a combinatory game of the essences of masculinity and femininity. Such that, to put it in contemporary gender studies parlance, the social production of norms and their subsequent descriptions finds a ready-made ontological division, ready to essentialize “masculinity” and “femininity” immediately. Traditional ontology was thus always also a machine for producing “masculine” and “feminine” essences, or, more precisely, for grounding these essences in being.

女性主义和性别研究之所以认为这种对性差异的本体论化问题很大，理由很明显。性差异一旦被加固到本体论层面，就牢牢锚定在本质主义之中——它变成了一场男性特质与女性特质诸本质的组合游戏。于是，用当代性别研究的说法，规范的社会生产及其随后的描述，碰上了一个现成的本体论划分，可以立刻把“男性特质”和“女性特质”本质化。因此，传统本体论也一直是一台生产“男性”和“女性”本质的机器，更准确地说，是一台把这些本质奠基在存在之中的机器。

When modern science broke with this ontology, it also mostly broke with ontology tout court. (Modern) science is not ontology; it neither pretends to make ontological claims nor, from a critical perspective on science, recognizes that it is nevertheless making them.

当现代科学与这种本体论决裂时，它大体上也同本体论本身决裂了。（现代）科学不是本体论；它既不假装提出本体论主张，从批判科学的视角看，也不承认自己其实仍在提出这类主张。

Perhaps more surprisingly, modern philosophy also mostly broke not only with traditional ontology but also with ontology tout court. Immanuel Kant is the name most strongly associated with this break: If one can have no knowledge about things in themselves, the classical ontological question of being qua being seems to lose its ground. It is a fact, however, that the ontological debate, after a considerable time of withdrawal from the foreground of the philosophical (theoretical) stage—and, perhaps even more importantly, of not appealing to the general interest—is now making a massive “return” to this stage, with an outburst of “new ontologies.” To be sure, these are diverse and sometimes very different philosophical projects. But it is safe to say that for none of them does sexual difference (in any form) play any part in their ontological considerations.

也许更令人惊讶的是，现代哲学大体上不仅与传统本体论断裂，也与本体论本身断裂。与这一断裂联系得最紧的名字是伊曼努尔·康德：如果人不能对物自体有任何知识，那么“存在之为存在”这个古典本体论问题似乎就失去了根基。3_2 然而事实是，本体论争论在相当长一段时间里退出了哲学（理论）舞台的前台——或许更重要的是，它不再吸引普遍兴趣——如今却随着“新本体论”的爆发，大规模地“返回”这一舞台。当然，这些是多样的、有时彼此非常不同的哲学计划。但可以稳妥地说，在它们当中，没有任何一个让性差异（无论以何种形式）在其本体论考量中发挥作用。

note：感觉就是挪用了存在与时间的第一节。

Since we are debating psychoanalysis and sexual difference, implicating Freud and Lacan in the discussion of the ontological dimension of sexual difference might look like the summit of possible oddities. For it seems not only to go against the numerous and outstanding efforts the defenders of psychoanalysis have, for decades, invested in showing the incompatibility of psychoanalysis with any kind of sexual essentialism; it is also contrary to what both Freud and Lacan thought and said about ontology. In view of the desexualization of reality that occurred with the Galilean revolution in science mentioned above, psychoanalysis (at least in its Freudian-Lacanian vein) is far from lamenting this. Its diagnosis of Western civilization is not one of the “forgetting of the sexual,” and it does not see itself as something that will bring the sexual coloring of the universe back into focus again, as if reenchanting it (sexually). On the contrary, it sees itself (and its “object”) as strictly coextensive with the move of desexualization.

[Psychoanalysis] proceeds from the same status as Science itself. It is engaged in the central lack in which the subject experiences itself as desire. … It has nothing to forget [a reference, no doubt, to the Heideggerian “forgetting of Being”], for it implies no recognition of any substance on which it claims to operate, even that of sexuality. (Lacan 1987, 266)

既然我们正在讨论精神分析与性差异，那么把弗洛伊德和拉康牵扯进性差异的本体论维度，看起来可能是最离奇不过的事。因为这似乎不仅违背了精神分析捍卫者数十年来投入的大量卓越努力——他们一直想表明精神分析与任何形式的性本质主义不相容；也违背弗洛伊德和拉康关于本体论的想法和说法。鉴于上文提到的伽利略科学革命所造成的现实的去性化，精神分析（至少在其弗洛伊德-拉康脉络中）远不会为此悲叹。它对西方文明的诊断并不是“对性的遗忘”，它也不把自己看作某种会把宇宙的性色彩重新拉回焦点、仿佛要（在性方面）给宇宙重新赋魅的东西。相反，它把自己（以及它的“对象”）看作与去性化运动严格同延。

精神分析从与科学本身相同的地位出发。它投身于那个中心性缺失，主体在其中把自身经验为欲望。……它没有什么可遗忘的［无疑是指海德格尔式的“对存在的遗忘”］，因为它并不承认任何它声称要在其上操作的实体，哪怕是性实体。（Lacan 1987, 266）

I am not making this point, however, in order to argue that psychoanalysis is in fact much less centered on the sexual than is commonly assumed, or to promote the “culturalized version” of psychoanalysis. Rather, the sexual in psychoanalysis is something very different from the sense-making combinatory game—it is precisely something that disrupts this game and makes it impossible. What one needs to see and grasp, to begin with, is where the real divide runs here. Psychoanalysis is both coextensive with this desexualization of reality, in the sense of breaking with ontology and science as a sexual technique or sexual combinatory, and absolutely uncompromising when it comes to the sexual as the irreducible Real (not substance). There is no contradiction here, just as there is no contradiction in the opposite, Jungian “revisionist” stance, which articulates a total culturalization of the sexual (its transcription into cultural archetypes) while also maintaining the principle of ontological combinatory (of two fundamental principles, yin and yang). The lesson and the imperative of psychoanalysis is not “Let us devote all our attention to the sexual (meaning) as our ultimate horizon”; it is instead a reduction of sex and the sexual (which, in fact, has always been overloaded with meanings and interpretations) to the point of ontological inconsistency, which, as such, is irreducible.

不过，我这样说，并不是要论证精神分析实际上远不像通常以为的那样以性为中心，也不是要推销精神分析的“文化化版本”。恰恰相反，精神分析所说的性，和那种生产意义的组合游戏非常不同——它恰恰会打断这个游戏，使这个游戏不可能成立。首先需要看到并把握的是：真正的分界线在这里。精神分析一方面与这种现实的去性化同延：也就是与作为性技术或性的组合的本体论和科学决裂；另一方面，当性被当作不可化约的实在界（而不是实体）时，它又绝不妥协。这里没有矛盾；正如在相反的荣格式“修正主义”立场中也没有矛盾：这种立场一方面把性彻底文化化（把性转写为文化原型），另一方面又坚持本体论组合的原则（即阴阳两个基本原则）。精神分析的教训和命令并不是“让我们把全部注意力都献给作为终极视域的性（意义）”；相反，它要把性以及性的东西（事实上，它们一直背负着过多的意义和解释）还原到本体论不一致的点上，而这种不一致本身是不可化约的。

Lacan’s emphatic claim that psychoanalysis is not a new ontology (a sexual ontology, for example) is thus not something that we are going to contest. But we will also not take the stance according to which psychoanalysis simply does not (and would not) have anything to do with ontology. The stakes are much higher, and the psychoanalytic relationship to philosophy (as ontology) is much more interesting and intricate. Perhaps the best way of putting it would be to say that their non-relationship, implied in “psychoanalysis is not an ontology,” is of the most intimate kind. I hope this statement will justify itself in what follows.

因此，拉康着重断言精神分析不是一种新的本体论（比如一种性的本体论），这一点我们并不打算反驳。但我们也不会采取这样的立场：精神分析根本与本体论没有任何关系（将来也不会有）。这里的赌注要大得多，而精神分析与哲学（作为本体论）的关系也更有意思、更错综复杂。也许最好的说法是：“精神分析不是一种本体论”所暗示的那种非关系，恰恰是最亲密的一种非关系。我希望这个说法会在下文中证明自己站得住脚。

One of the conceptual deadlocks in simply emphasizing that gender is an entirely social, or cultural, construction is that it remains within the dichotomy nature/culture. Judith Butler saw this very clearly, which is why her project radicalizes this theory by linking it to the theory of performativity. As opposed to expressivity, indicating a preexistence and independence of that which is being expressed, performativity refers in Butler’s account to actions that create, so to speak, the essences that they express. Nothing here preexists: sociosymbolic practices of different discourses and their antagonisms create the very “essences,” or phenomena, that they regulate. The time and the dynamics of repetition that this creation requires open up the only margin of freedom (to possibly change or influence this process). What differentiates this concept of performativity from the classical, linguistic one is precisely the element of time: it is not that the performative gesture creates a new reality immediately, that is, in the very act of being performed (like the performative utterance “I declare this session open”); rather, it refers to a process in which sociosymbolic constructions, by dint of repetition and reiteration, become nature—“only natural,” as the saying goes. What is referred to as natural is the sedimentation of the discursive, and in this view the dialectics of nature and culture becomes the internal dialectics of culture. Culture both produces and regulates (what is referred to as) “nature.” We are no longer dealing with two terms: sociosymbolic activity, and something on which it is performed; instead, we are dealing with something like an internal dialectics of the One (the discursive) that not only models things but also creates the things it models, which opens up a certain depth of field. Performativity is thus a kind of onto-logy of the discursive, responsible for both the logos and the being of things.

仅仅强调性别完全由社会或文化建构，其概念死结之一在于：这种说法仍然困在自然/文化的二分之中。朱迪斯·巴特勒对此看得很清楚，3_3所以她的方案把这一理论激进化，将其与操演性理论联系起来。与表达性相对——表达性意味着被表达之物预先存在并且独立——在巴特勒的论述中，操演性指的是这样一些行动：它们可以说创造了它们所表达的那些本质。这里没有任何东西预先存在：不同话语及其对抗的社会象征实践，创造了它们所规制的那些“本质”或现象。这种创造所需要的时间和重复的动力，打开了唯一一点自由余地（有可能改变或影响这一过程）。这一操演性概念与经典语言学操演性概念的区别，恰恰在于时间要素：操演姿态并不是立刻创造一个新现实，也就是说，不是在被执行的行动本身中创造（像操演话语“我宣布本次会议开幕”那样）；相反，它指的是一个过程：社会象征建构通过反复和重述而成为自然——也就是俗话说的“自然而然”。所谓自然，是话语性的沉积；在这种视角下，自然与文化的辩证法就变成文化的内部辩证法。文化既生产也规制（所谓的）“自然”。我们不再面对两个项：社会象征活动，以及它作用于其上的某个东西；相反，我们面对的是类似一者（话语性的）的内部辩证法，它不仅模塑事物，也创造它所模塑的事物，这就开启了一定的景深。因此，操演性是话语的一种本体-逻辑学，既对事物的逻各斯负责，也对事物的存在负责。

To a great extent, Lacanian psychoanalysis seems compatible with this account, and it is often presented as such. The primacy of the signifier and of the field of the Other, language as constitutive of reality and of the unconscious (including the dialectics of desire), the creationist aspect of the symbolic and its dialectics (with notions such as symbolic causality, symbolic efficiency, materiality of the signifier) … all of these (undisputed) claims notwithstanding, Lacan’s position is irreducibly different from the performative ontology described above. In what way, exactly? And what is the status of the Real that Lacan insists upon when speaking of sexuality?

在很大程度上，拉康派精神分析似乎与这一论述相容，而且它也常被这样呈现。尽管有这些（无可争议的）主张——能指和大他者场域的优先性，语言构成现实和无意识（包括欲望的辩证法），象征界的创造论面向及其辩证法（例如象征因果性、象征效力、能指的物质性等概念）——拉康的立场仍与上述操演本体论不可化约地不同。究竟在哪些方面不同？拉康在谈论性时坚持的实在界，其地位又是什么？

It is not simply that Lacan has to take into account and make room for the other, “vital” part of the psychoanalytic notions (such as the libido, the drive, the sexualized body), which would be defined as “real,” as opposed to belonging to the symbolic. This kind of parlance, and the perspective it implies, are very misleading. Something else is at stake: taking the signifying order as his starting point, Lacan sees it as the locus of a fundamental split. While the signifying order creates its own space and the beings that populate it (which roughly corresponds to the space of performativity described above), something else gets added to it. It could be said that this something is parasitic on performative productivity; it is not produced by the signifying gesture, but together with and “on top of” it. It is inseparable from this gesture, but, unlike what we call discursive entities/beings, not created by it. It is neither a symbolic entity nor one constituted by the symbolic; rather, it is collateral for the symbolic. Moreover, it is not a being: it is discernible only as a (disruptive) effect within the symbolic field, its disturbance, its bias. In other words, the emergence of the signifier is not reducible to, or exhausted by, the symbolic. The signifier does not only produce a new, symbolic reality (including its own materiality, causality, and laws); it also “produces” the dimension that Lacan calls the Real, which is related to the points of structural impossibility/contradiction of symbolic reality itself. This is what irredeemably stains the symbolic, stains its supposed purity, and accounts for the fact that the symbolic game of pure differentiality is always a game with loaded dice. This is the very space, or dimension, that sustains the “vital” phenomena mentioned above (the libido or jouissance, the drive, the sexualized body) in their out-of-jointness with the symbolic.

情况并不只是说，拉康必须考虑精神分析概念中另一个“生命性”部分（比如力比多、冲动、性化身体），并给它们腾出位置；这些部分会被界定为“实在界”，而非属于象征界。这种说法以及它暗示的视角非常有误导性。真正的问题在别处：拉康以能指秩序为起点，把它看作一个根本性分裂的场所。能指秩序创造出自己的空间以及填充其中的存在者（大致对应上文所说的操演性空间），但还有别的东西被附加到它上面。可以说，这个东西寄生于操演性生产；它不是由能指姿态生产出来的，而是与这个姿态一起、并且“压在”它上面出现的。它和这个姿态不可分离，但与我们所称的话语实体/存在者不同，它不是由这个姿态创造的。它既不是象征性实体，也不是由象征界构成的东西；更准确地说，它是象征界的附带产物。而且，它不是一个存在者：它只能作为象征界领域内的一种（扰乱性）效果、作为它的扰动、它的偏向而被辨认出来。换言之，能指的出现不能被还原为象征界，也不能被象征界耗尽。能指不仅生产出一个新的象征性现实（包括它自身的物质性、因果性和法则），它还“生产”出拉康称为实在界的维度；这个维度关涉象征性现实本身的结构不可能性/矛盾点。正是这个东西不可挽回地玷污了象征界，玷污了它所谓的纯粹性，并解释了这一点：纯粹差异性的象征游戏始终是一场掷灌铅骰子的游戏。也正是这个空间或维度，支撑着前面提到的那些“生命性”现象（力比多或享乐、冲动、性化身体），使它们与象征界保持错位。

note：实在界只能由我们根据象征界的匮乏回溯性推演出来。

So: the something produced by the signifier, in addition to what it produces as its field, magnetizes this field in a certain way. It is responsible for the fact that the symbolic field, or the field of the Other, is never neutral (or structured by pure differentality), but conflictual, asymmetrical, “not-all,” ridden with a fundamental antagonism. In other words, the antagonism of the discursive field is not due to the fact that this field is always “composed” of multiple elements, or multiple multiples, competing among themselves and not properly unified; it refers to the very space in which these different multiples exist. Just as, for Marx, “class antagonism” is not simply conflict between different classes but the very principle of the constitution of class society, antagonism as such never simply exists between conflicting parties; it is the very structuring principle of this conflict, and of the elements involved in it.

因此：能指所生产出来的那个“某物”，除了生产出它自身的场域之外，还以某种方式把这个场域磁化。正是它导致象征场域，也就是大他者的场域，从来不是中立的（也不是由纯粹差异性结构起来的），而是冲突性的、不对称的、“非全”的，贯穿着一种根本性对抗。换言之，话语场域的对抗，并不只是因为该场域总是由多个要素、或多个“多”所“组成”，这些要素彼此竞争、从未被恰当地统一起来；它指向的是这些不同的“多”得以存在的那个空间本身。正如在马克思那里，“阶级对抗”并不只是不同阶级之间的冲突，而是阶级社会构成的根本原则；对抗本身也从来不只是存在于冲突各方之间；它就是这一冲突、以及卷入其中的诸要素的结构性原则本身。

note：也并非先有大他者，能指由大他者生产出来，而是能指产生后回溯性推理出大他者。

Yet this account demands a further specification, which makes it slightly more complex. For we could ask: But how is it that the signifier “produces” something on top of what it produces as its (properly signifying) field? Why does this happen? And the answer (with which we come back to a crucial point that I have already introduced) is: because the signifying structure is coextensive with a gap.

Discourse begins from the fact that there is a gap here. … But, after all, nothing prevents us from saying that it is because discourse begins that the gap is produced. It is a matter of complete indifference toward the result. What is certain is that discourse is implied in the gap. (Lacan 2006b, 107)

不过，这一叙述还需要进一步说明，因而会稍微更复杂一点。因为我们可以问：可是，能指究竟如何在它作为其（严格意义上的能指）场域所生产的东西之上，还“生产”出某种东西呢？为什么会这样？答案（借此我们回到我已经引入的一个关键点）是：因为能指结构与一个缺口同延。

话语始于这里有一个裂隙这一事实。……但毕竟，没有什么阻止我们说：正是因为话语开始，裂隙才被生产出来。对结果来说，这完全无关紧要。确定的是，话语被隐含在裂隙之中。（Lacan 2006b, 107）

This implication of discursivity and gap is a crucial point, or at least the point that I will take as crucial in my argument. Lacan’s writing of it, with which we have become familiar, is this: $S(Ⱥ)$, referring to a constitutive lack in the Other. What I would like to emphasize is the dimension of something like virtual subtraction or “minus” involved in this notion. This emphasis allows us to say not only that the signifying order is inconsistent and incomplete, but, in a stronger and more paradoxical phrasing, that the signifying order emerges as already lacking one signifier, that it appears with the lack of a signifier “built into it,” so to speak (a signifier which, if it existed, would be the “binary signifier”). In this precise sense the signifying order could be said to begin, not with One (nor with multiplicity), but with a “minus one”—and we shall return to this crucial point in more detail later on. It is in the place of this gap or negativity that appears the surplus-enjoyment which stains the signifying structure: the heterogeneous element pertaining to the signifying structure, yet irreducible to it.

话语性与裂隙之间的这种蕴含关系是一个关键点，至少是我在论证中会当作关键的点。拉康对它的写法我们已经很熟悉，就是 $S(Ⱥ)$，指的是大他者中的构成性缺失。我想强调的是，这个观念中包含着某种类似虚拟减法或“减号”的维度。这样的强调让我们不仅可以说能指秩序是不一致且不完整的，而且可以用更强、也更悖论的说法说：能指秩序一出现就已经缺了一个能指，可以说它出现时就把“缺少一个能指”内置其中了（这个能指如果存在，就会是“二元能指”）。正是在这个确切意义上，可以说能指秩序不是从“一”（也不是从“多”）开始，而是从“负一”开始——后文我们会更详细地回到这个关键点。正是在这个裂隙或否定性的位置上，出现了沾染能指结构的剩余享乐：它属于能指结构，却又不可还原为能指结构。

note：任何一个集合都需要有一个东西被排除，能指这个大的宇集合里被排除的就是阳具能指，所以能指集合能被定义出来，然后主人能指是率领这个能指集合的第一能指，然后它生产了知识能指$S_2$，让我们可以使用/认识这个能指集合。

We could also say: The emergence of the signifying order directly coincides with the non-emergence of one signifier, and this fact (this original minus one) leaves its trace in a particular feature/disturbance of the signifying system—enjoyment. So it is not so much that the signifier “produces” this surplus as that this surplus is the way in which the lack of the (binary) signifier exists within the discursive structure and marks it in certain determinable ways. It marks (and thus effectively “curves”) it by sticking to a certain set (or chain) of other signifiers that relate in some way to this lack of the signifier. The way enjoyment relates to (or is linked to) the signifying order passes through what is missing in this order; it does not relate to it directly, but via its constitutive negativity (a minus one). This negativity is the Real of the junction between the (missing) signifier and enjoyment; and the conceptual name for this configuration in psychoanalysis is sexuality (or the sexual). Sexuality is coextensive with the effect of the signifying gap, at the place of which surplus-enjoyment emerges, on the rest of the signifying chain (including bodily erogenous zones which are certainly not independent of the signifying grid).

我们也可以说：能指秩序的出现，与一个能指的未出现直接重合；而这个事实（这个原初的减一）在能指系统的某个特殊特征/紊乱中留下痕迹——享乐。所以，与其说能指“生产”了这个剩余，不如说这个剩余是（二元）能指的缺失在话语结构内部存在的方式，并以某些可确定的方式标记了该结构。它通过附着于某一组（或一条链）其他能指来标记（并因此有效地“弯曲”）这个结构；这些其他能指以某种方式与能指的这种缺失相关。享乐与能指秩序发生关系（或与之相联）的方式，要经由这个秩序中缺失的东西；它并不直接与秩序相关，而是通过秩序的构成性否定性（一个减一）来相关。这种否定性就是（缺失的）能指与享乐之间接合处的实在界；在精神分析中，这一构型的概念名称就是性（或性的东西）。性（或性的东西）与能指缺口作用于能指链其余部分的效果同延；剩余享乐正是在这个缺口的位置上出现，而能指链的其余部分包括身体快感区——它们当然并不独立于能指网格。

Sexuality is not some being that exists beyond the symbolic; it “exists” solely as the contradiction of the symbolic space that appears because of the constitutively missing signifier, and of what appears at its place (enjoyment).

性欲并不是某种存在于象征界之外的存在物；它仅仅“存在”为象征空间的矛盾——这一矛盾之所以出现，是因为那个构成性缺失的能指，以及出现在它位置上的东西（享乐）。

It would thus be wrong to say that the signifier of the sexual is missing; the sexual is not some extradiscursive object lacking a signifier; rather, it is a direct consequence (“extension”) of the missing of a signifier, that is, of the gap with which the signifying order emerges. This is why sexuality is neither something outside of the signifying order (which this order would strive to represent fully, but fail), nor does it have a signifier. To spell it out in full: human sexuality is the placeholder of the missing signifier. It is a mess, but it is a mess that actually compensates for the sexual relation as impossible (to be written). This, I believe, is a crucial reversal of the common perception that we need to make: the messiness of our sexuality is not a consequence or result of there being no sexual relation, it is not that our sexuality is messy because it is without a clear signifying rule; it emerges only from, and at the place of, this lack, and attempts to deal with it. Sexuality is not ravaged by, or disturbed, because of a gap cutting deep into its “tissue,” it is, rather, the messy sewing up of this gap. Lacan actually says as much in a rather offhand remark in the Seminar … ou pire, but it is absolutely crucial: the four formulas known as the formulas of sexuation are his attempt to “fix that which makes up for [supplée à] what I have called the impossibility to write the sexual relation” (Lacan 2011, 138). In other words, sex is messy because it appears at the point of the breaking down of the signifying consistency, or logic (its point of impossibility), not because it is in itself illogical and messy: its messiness is the result of the attempt to invent a logic at the very point of the impasse of such logic. Its “irrationality” is the summit of its efforts to establish a sexual “rationale.” This, at least, is how Lacan conceived of the formulas of sexuation: they (re)state the issue of sexuality and “sexual relations” as a logical problem (problem of the signifying logic) out of which it springs.

因此，说性的能指是缺失的，这是错的；性并不是某种外在于话语、缺少能指的客体；相反，它是能指之缺失的直接后果（“延伸”），也就是说，是能指秩序借以出现的那个裂隙的直接后果。这就是为什么性既不是能指秩序之外的东西（能指秩序会努力把它完整地表象出来，却会失败），也不拥有一个能指。把话说全：人的性就是那个缺失能指的占位符。它是一团乱，但这是一团实际上补偿了“性关系不可能（被书写）”的乱。我认为，我们需要做出的一个关键颠倒就在于此：我们性的混乱并不是“没有性关系”的后果或结果；并不是说，我们的性因为没有清楚的能指规则而混乱；它只从这一缺失中、且在这一缺失的位置上出现，并试图处理这一缺失。性并不是因为一道裂隙深深切入其“组织”而被绞缠或扰乱；相反，性是对这一裂隙的杂乱缝合。拉康其实在研讨班《……或更糟》中一句相当随口的评论里就说过这一点，但这一点绝对关键：那四个被称为性化公式的公式，正是他试图“固定那弥补[增补，supplée à]我所称的‘书写性关系之不可能’的东西”（Lacan 2011, 138）。换句话说，性之所以混乱，是因为它出现在能指一致性或逻辑崩解的点上（即其不可能性的点），而不是因为它自身就是不合逻辑、混乱的：它的混乱，是在这种逻辑陷入僵局的那一点上发明一种逻辑的尝试所造成的结果。它的“非理性”正是它努力建立一种性的“理据”的顶点。至少，这就是拉康构想性化公式的方式：它们（重新）把性和“性关系”的问题陈述为一个逻辑问题（能指逻辑的问题），而性正是从这一问题中涌现出来的。

If we now return to the question of what all this implies in relation to ontology in general, and, more specifically, to the performative ontology of contemporary gender studies, we must start from the following crucial implication: Lacan is led to establish a difference between being and the Real. The Real is not a being, or a substance, but its deadlock, the point of its impossibility. It is inseparable from being, yet it is not being. One could say that for psychoanalysis, there is no being independent of language (or discourse)—which is why it often seems compatible with contemporary forms of nominalism. All being is symbolic; it is being in the Other. But with a crucial addition, which could be formulated as follows: there is only being in the symbolic—except that there is the Real. There “is” the Real, but this Real is no being. Yet it is not simply the outside of being; it is not something besides being, it is a convulsion, a stumbling block of the space of being. It exists only as the inherent contradiction of (symbolic) being. This, and nothing else, is at stake when psychoanalysis links sex with the Real. As Joan Copjec put it in her seminal text on these questions:

When we speak of language’s failure with respect to sex, we speak not of its falling short of a prediscursive object but of its falling into contradiction with itself. Sex coincides with this failure, this inevitable contradiction. Sex is, then, the impossibility of completing meaning, not (as Butler’s historicist/deconstructionalist argument would have it) a meaning that is incomplete, instable. Or, the point is that sex is the structural incompleteness of language, not that sex is itself incomplete. (Copjec 1994, 206)

如果我们现在回到这个问题：这一切相对于一般本体论，尤其相对于当代性别研究中的述行本体论，究竟意味着什么，那么我们必须从下面这个关键意涵出发：拉康由此确立起存在与实在界之间的区分。实在界不是一种存在，也不是实体，而是存在的死结，是存在的不可能之点。它与存在不可分离，但它并不是存在。可以说，对精神分析而言，不存在独立于语言（或话语）的存在——这也是为什么它常显得与当代各种唯名论相容。一切存在都是象征性的；它是大他者中的存在。但还要加一个关键补充，可以这样表述：只有在象征界中才有存在——只不过，还有实在界。“有”实在界，但这个实在界并不是存在。然而，它并不单纯是存在的外部；它也不是存在之外的某个东西，它是存在空间的痉挛，是存在空间的绊脚石。它只作为（象征性）存在的内在矛盾而存在。当精神分析把性与实在界联系起来时，关键就在于此，别无其他。正如 Joan Copjec 在她讨论这些问题的奠基性文本中所说：

当我们谈论语言在性上的失败时，我们说的不是语言未能抵达某个前话语对象，而是语言陷入了与自身的矛盾。性恰好与这种失败、这种不可避免的矛盾重合。因此，性就是意义之完成的不可能性，而不是（如 Butler 的历史主义/解构主义论证所主张的那样）一种不完整、不稳定的意义。或者说，关键在于：性是语言的结构性不完整，而不是性本身不完整。（Copjec 1994, 206）
